At the same time, lower ratings for reliability and predictability point to important technical and design challenges for future iterations. The findings suggest that challenge-based, LLM-enabled humanoid interaction can serve as a promising and replicable method for robotics awareness in industrial and operational environments.

</details>


### [181] [From Noise to Intent: Anchoring Generative VLA Policies with Residual Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21391)
*Yiming Zhong,Yaoyu He,Zemin Yang,Pengfei Tian,Yifan Huang,Qingqiu Huang,Xinge Zhu,Yuexin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ResVLA提出了一种"从意图精炼"的新范式，通过频谱分析将机器人控制解耦为确定性低频锚点（全局意图）和随机性高频残差（局部动态），并利用残差扩散桥仅精炼局部动态，从而弥合高层语义理解与低层物理控制之间的跨尺度鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式VLA策略采用"从噪声生成"范式，忽略了认知与动作之间固有的时空尺度不匹配问题，导致表示效率低下且优化过程中条件对齐弱。

Method: ResVLA利用频谱分析将控制信号解耦为确定性低频锚点（全局意图）和随机性高频残差（局部动态），以预测的意图为锚点，通过残差扩散桥仅对局部动态进行精炼。

Result: 在仿真实验中，ResVLA达到了具有竞争力的性能，对语言和机器人形态扰动具有强鲁棒性，且收敛速度优于标准生成式基线；在真实机器人实验中也展现了强劲表现。

Conclusion: 将生成范式从"从噪声生成"转向"从意图精炼"，通过频谱解耦和残差扩散桥有效解决了认知-动作跨尺度问题，在效率和鲁棒性上均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Bridging high-level semantic understanding with low-level physical control remains a persistent challenge in embodied intelligence, stemming from the fundamental spatiotemporal scale mismatch between cognition and action. Existing generative VLA policies typically adopt a "Generation-from-Noise" paradigm, which disregards this disparity, leading to representation inefficiency and weak condition alignment during optimization. In this work, we propose ResVLA, an architecture that shifts the paradigm to "Refinement-from-Intent." Recognizing that robotic motion naturally decomposes into global intent and local dynamics, ResVLA utilizes spectral analysis to decouple control into a deterministic low-frequency anchor and a stochastic high-frequency residual. By anchoring the generative process on the predicted intent, our model focuses strictly on refining local dynamics via a residual diffusion bridge. Extensive simulation experiments show that ResVLA achieves competitive performance, strong robustness to language and robot embodiment perturbations, and faster convergence than standard generative baselines. It also demonstrates strong performance in real-world robot experiments.

</details>


### [182] [Ufil: A Unified Framework for Infrastructure-based Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21471)
*Simon Schäfer,Lucas Hegerath,Marius Molz,Massimo Marcon,Bassam Alrifaee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Ufil，一个统一的 infrastructure-based 定位框架，提供标准化的对象模型和可复用的多目标跟踪组件。通过融合三种异构数据源（ETSI ITS-G5 CAM消息、激光雷达路侧节点、路面感应层），在CARLA仿真器和CAV测试平台上实现了车道级的横向定位精度（平均RMSE约0.3m）和低于100ms的端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基础设施定位系统存在碎片化、应用特定化的问题，其感知、跟踪和中间件紧密耦合，导致研究人员难以改进单个组件而无须重新实现整个流水线。需要统一的框架来解耦各模块，提升开发效率和复用性。

Method: 设计标准化对象模型和可复用多目标跟踪组件，提供预测、检测、关联、状态更新和轨迹管理的接口与参考实现；采用C++/ROS 2开源实现；融合三种异质数据源（车载ITS-G5 CAM、激光雷达、路面感应层）；采用与规模无关的执行模型，可在CARLA仿真器和CAV测试平台上无修改运行。

Result: 在三车道高速公路场景中（仿真423车/测试台355车），融合系统的平均横向位置RMSE在CARLA中为0.31m，在CPM Lab中为0.29m；平均绝对朝向误差约2.2°；各模态的中位端到端延迟均低于100ms。

Conclusion: Ufil通过统一框架和标准化接口有效解耦了基础设施定位系统的各组件，实现了跨平台、跨模态的可复用性和优异的定位精度与实时性，为基础设施定位研究提供了开源基础平台。

Abstract: Infrastructure-based localization enhances road safety and traffic management by providing state estimates of road users. Development is hindered by fragmented, application-specific stacks that tightly couple perception, tracking, and middleware. We introduce Ufil, a Unified Framework for Infrastructure-Based Localization with a standardized object model and reusable multi-object tracking components. Ufil offers interfaces and reference implementations for prediction, detection, association, state update, and track management, allowing researchers to improve components without reimplementing the pipeline. Ufil is open-source C++/ROS 2 software with documentation and executable examples. We demonstrate Ufil by integrating three heterogeneous data sources into a single localization pipeline combining (i) vehicle onboard units broadcasting ETSI ITS-G5 Cooperative Awareness Messages, (ii) a lidar-based roadside sensor node, and (iii) an in-road sensitive surface layer. The pipeline runs unchanged in the CARLA simulator and a small-scale CAV testbed, demonstrating Ufil's scale-independent execution model. In a three-lane highway scenario with 423 and 355 vehicles in simulation and testbed, respectively, the fused system achieves lane-level lateral accuracy with mean lateral position RMSEs of 0.31 m in CARLA and 0.29 m in the CPM Lab, and mean absolute orientation errors around 2.2°. Median end-to-end latencies from sensing to fused output remain below 100 ms across all modalities in both environments.

</details>


### [183] [MISTY: High-Throughput Motion Planning via Mixer-based Single-step Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21489)
*Yining Xing,Zehong Ke,Yiqian Tu,Zhiyuan Liu,Wenhao Yu,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MISTY提出了一种基于MLP-Mixer和单步推理的高通量生成式运动规划器，通过隐空间漂移损失将分布演化转移至训练阶段，在nuPlan基准上以99+ FPS实现SOTA闭环性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的轨迹规划器因迭代神经函数评估导致高推理延迟，无法满足自动驾驶对实时性的严苛要求。

Method: 1) 向量化子图编码器捕捉环境上下文；2) VAE将专家轨迹压缩至32维隐空间；3) 超轻量MLP-Mixer解码器消除二次注意力复杂度；4) 隐空间漂移损失通过显式吸引力/排斥力在训练阶段完成分布演化，使模型具备合成主动超车等新颖机动能力。

Result: 在nuPlan Test14-hard分上，非反应式和反应式场景综合得分分别达80.32和82.21；端到端延迟10.1ms，帧率超99 FPS，比迭代扩散规划器快一个数量级。

Conclusion: MISTY通过单步推理和隐空间漂移机制，在保持生成多样性的同时实现了数量级的推理加速，验证了非自回归生成范式在自动驾驶运动规划中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-modal trajectory generation is essential for safe autonomous driving, yet existing diffusion-based planners suffer from high inference latency due to iterative neural function evaluations. This paper presents MISTY (Mixer-based Inference for Single-step Trajectory-drifting Yield), a high-throughput generative motion planner that achieves state-of-the-art closed-loop performance with pure single-step inference. MISTY integrates a vectorized Sub-Graph encoder to capture environment context, a Variational Autoencoder to structure expert trajectories into a compact 32-dimensional latent manifold, and an ultra-lightweight MLP-Mixer decoder to eliminate quadratic attention complexity. Importantly, we introduce a latent-space drifting loss that shifts the complex distribution evolution entirely to the training phase. By formulating explicit attractive and repulsive forces, this mechanism empowers the model to synthesize novel, proactive maneuvers, such as active overtaking, that are virtually absent from the raw expert demonstrations. Extensive evaluations on the nuPlan benchmark demonstrate that MISTY achieves state-of-the-art results on the challenging Test14-hard split, with comprehensive scores of 80.32 and 82.21 in non-reactive and reactive settings, respectively. Operating at over 99 FPS with an end-to-end latency of 10.1 ms, MISTY offers an order-of-magnitude speedup over iterative diffusion planners while while achieving significantly robust generation.

</details>


### [184] [X2-N: A Transformable Wheel-legged Humanoid Robot with Dual-mode Locomotion and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21541)
*Yan Ning,Xingzhou Chen,Delong Li,Hao Zhang,Hanfu Gai,Tongyuan Li,Cheng Zhang,Zhihui Peng,Ling Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X2-N是一款高自由度变形机器人，支持人形与轮腿双模式无缝切换，通过强化学习实现统一控制，在动态滑行、爬楼梯、包裹递送等任务中展现了高效运动、强地形适应性和稳定操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轮腿机器人通常采用固定轮式脚和有限自由度髋部，导致腿部运动时稳定性不足；且多数平台缺少完整上肢，限制了灵巧操作能力。X2-N旨在同时解决运动与操作的双重局限。

Method: 设计了高自由度变形机器人X2-N，支持人形与轮腿双模式及其关节重构式切换；提出了基于强化学习的全身控制框架，实现混合运动、变形与操作的统一控制。

Result: 在动态滑行、爬楼梯、包裹递送等任务中验证了X2-N的高运动效率、强地形适应性和稳定的运动-操作协同性能。

Conclusion: X2-N在运动效率、地形适应性和运动-操作协同方面表现优异，展示了在实际环境中部署的潜力。

Abstract: Wheel-legged robots combine the efficiency of wheeled locomotion with the versatility of legged systems, enabling rapid traversal over both continuous and discrete terrains. However, conventional designs typically employ fixed wheels as feet and limited degrees of freedom (DoFs) at the hips, resulting in reduced stability and mobility during legged locomotion compared to humanoids with flat feet. In addition, most existing platforms lack a full upper body with arms, which limits their ability to perform dexterous manipulation tasks.
  In this letter, we present X2-N, a high-DoF transformable robot with dual-mode locomotion and manipulation. X2-N can operate in both humanoid and wheel-legged forms and transform seamlessly between them through joint reconfiguration. We further propose a reinforcement learning (RL)-based whole-body control framework tailored to this morphology, enabling unified control across hybrid locomotion, transformation, and manipulation. We validate X2-N in a range of challenging locomotion and manipulation tasks, including dynamic skating-like motion, stair climbing and package delivery. Results demonstrate high locomotion efficiency, strong terrain adaptability, and stable loco-manipulation performance of X2-N, highlighting its potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [185] [SLAM as a Stochastic Control Problem with Partial Information: Optimal Solutions and Rigorous Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21693)
*Ilir Gusija,Fady Alajaji,Serdar Yüksel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文从最优随机控制视角重新审视主动SLAM问题，将其建模为非标准POMDP，引入编码状态几何信息的新型探索阶段代价函数，并在一般假设下推导出具有严格理论保证的近似最优解，通过数值实验验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将主动SLAM问题形式化为一个严格的最优随机控制问题，并为此类部分信息下的决策问题提供具有理论保证的近似最优解。

Method: 将主动SLAM建模为非标准POMDP，引入编码状态几何信息的新型探索阶段代价函数，在一般假设下分析相关正则性条件，并利用标准学习算法学习近优策略。

Result: 建立了具有严格理论保证的近似最优解，数值实验表明标准学习算法能够学习到近优策略。

Conclusion: 主动SLAM问题可以通过最优随机控制框架得到严格的理论建模，所提出的几何代价函数和近似求解方法在理论上可行且实践中有效。

Abstract: Simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM) is a foundational state estimation problem in robotics in which a robot accurately constructs a map of its environment while also localizing itself within this construction. We study the active SLAM problem through the lens of optimal stochastic control, thereby recasting it as a decision-making problem under partial information. After reviewing several commonly studied models, we present a general stochastic control formulation of active SLAM together with a rigorous treatment of motion, sensing, and map representation. We introduce a new exploration stage cost that encodes the geometry of the state when evaluating information-gathering actions. This formulation, constructed as a nonstandard partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), is then analyzed to derive rigorously justified approximate solutions that are near-optimal. To enable this analysis, the associated regularity conditions are studied under general assumptions that apply to a wide range of robotics applications. For a particular case, we conduct an extensive numerical study in which standard learning algorithms are used to learn near-optimal policies.

</details>


### [186] [A Compact Peristaltic Pump Based on Magneto-Elastic Hysteresis with Single Pneumatic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21729)
*Minjo Park,Metin Sitti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合单一气动输入与嵌入式被动磁体的软膜泵，通过磁弹滞后效应实现类蠕动运动，简化了传统蠕动泵的结构与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蠕动泵依赖机械、气动或磁驱动方式驱动膜变形，导致泵结构复杂、控制系统繁琐，亟需更简化的驱动方案。

Method: 通过单一气动输入与嵌入式被动磁体组合驱动软膜变形，建立理论模型分析驱动机制与系统动力学，利用数值模拟预测内部流体流动，并通过概念验证原型实验验证磁弹滞后行为。

Result: 数值模拟成功预测了磁弹滞后行为，且实验结果与模拟结果吻合，验证了所提出驱动机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的软膜泵通过单输入气动-磁耦合设计实现了简化的蠕动驱动，为工业、环境和生物医学领域的流体输运提供了一种结构更紧凑、控制更简便的新方案。

Abstract: Pumping fluids is fundamental to a wide range of industrial, environmental, and biomedical applications. Among various pumping mechanisms, peristaltic pumps enable efficient and safe fluid transport by deforming an elastic tube without direct contact with the working fluid. Although previous studies have introduced mechanical, pneumatic, or magnetic actuations to drive membrane deformation, these approaches often lead to complex pump architectures and control schemes. In this study, we present a soft membrane pump that achieves peristaltic motion through a single pneumatic input combined with an embedded passive magnet. The actuation mechanism and system dynamics were analyzed and simplified through modeling. Numerical simulations were conducted to predict the internal fluid flow, and the magneto-elastic hysteresis behavior observed in the simulations was successfully validated by experiments with a proof-of-concept prototype.

</details>


### [187] [Hi-WM: Human-in-the-World-Model for Scalable Robot Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21741)
*Yaxuan Li,Zhongyi Zhou,Yefei Chen,Yanjiang Guo,Jiaming Liu,Shanghang Zhang,Jianyu Chen,Yichen Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hi-WM提出一种基于世界模型的后训练框架，允许人类在世界模型的模拟环境中直接干预策略滚动过程，通过回滚和分支机制高效生成纠正轨迹，从而提升机器人策略的真实世界成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后训练方法依赖真实的物理执行，每次纠正都需要机器人运行时间、场景搭建、重置和操作员监督，成本高昂且难以扩展。因此，需要一种更高效、可扩展的后训练方式。

Method: Hi-WM框架包含：1）在世界模型内闭环 rollout 策略；2）当 rollout 出现错误或失败时，人类直接在世界模型中提供短时纠正动作；3）缓存中间状态并支持回滚和分支，使单个失败状态可被多次重用于不同的纠正延续；4）将生成的纠正轨迹加入训练集进行后训练。

Result: 在三个真实世界操作任务（包含刚体和可变形物体）以及两个策略主干上，Hi-WM相比基础策略平均提升37.9个百分点的成功率，相比世界模型闭环基线提升19.0个百分点。世界模型评估与真实世界性能高度相关（r=0.953）。

Conclusion: 世界模型不仅可以作为生成器或评估器，还可以作为有效的纠正基板，用于可扩展的机器人后训练。

Abstract: Post-training is essential for turning pretrained generalist robot policies into reliable task-specific controllers, but existing human-in-the-loop pipelines remain tied to physical execution: each correction requires robot time, scene setup, resets, and operator supervision in the real world. Meanwhile, action-conditioned world models have been studied mainly for imagination, synthetic data generation, and policy evaluation. We propose \textbf{Human-in-the-World-Model (Hi-WM)}, a post-training framework that uses a learned world model as a reusable corrective substrate for failure-targeted policy improvement. A policy is first rolled out in closed loop inside the world model; when the rollout becomes incorrect or failure-prone, a human intervenes directly in the model to provide short corrective actions. Hi-WM caches intermediate states and supports rollback and branching, allowing a single failure state to be reused for multiple corrective continuations and yielding dense supervision around behaviors that the base policy handles poorly. The resulting corrective trajectories are then added back to the training set for post-training. We evaluate Hi-WM on three real-world manipulation tasks spanning both rigid and deformable object interaction, and on two policy backbones. Hi-WM improves real-world success by 37.9 points on average over the base policy and by 19.0 points over a world-model closed-loop baseline, while world-model evaluation correlates strongly with real-world performance (r = 0.953). These results suggest that world models can serve not only as generators or evaluators, but also as effective corrective substrates for scalable robot post-training.

</details>


### [188] [Task-Driven Co-Design of Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21894)
*Maximilian Stralz,Meshal Alharbi,Yujun Huang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于单调协同设计理论的正式组合框架，用于任务驱动的异构多机器人系统协同设计，能够联合优化机器人设计、编队组成和规划，并通过案例研究展示了框架的灵活性、可扩展性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多集中于机器人设计、编队组成和规划等领域的孤立改进，缺乏考虑跨领域权衡和任务需求的系统级协同设计方法。

Method: 基于单调协同设计理论，将机器人、编队、规划器、执行器和评估器抽象为具有良好定义接口的互联设计问题，实现与具体实现和任务无关的联合优化。

Result: 通过系列案例研究证明，该框架可无缝集成多种组件模型（如新型机器人、任务配置文件和概率感知目标），并能系统性地发现非显而易见的设计替代方案，且具有最优性保证。

Conclusion: 该框架具有灵活性、可扩展性和可解释性，能够对复杂异构多机器人系统进行形式化协同设计推理。

Abstract: Designing multi-agent robotic systems requires reasoning across tightly coupled decisions spanning heterogeneous domains, including robot design, fleet composition, and planning. Much effort has been devoted to isolated improvements in these domains, whereas system-level co-design considering trade-offs and task requirements remains underexplored. In this work, we present a formal and compositional framework for the task-driven co-design of heterogeneous multi-robot systems. Building on a monotone co-design theory, we introduce general abstractions of robots, fleets, planners, executors, and evaluators as interconnected design problems with well-defined interfaces that are agnostic to both implementations and tasks. This structure enables efficient joint optimization of robot design, fleet composition, and planning under task-specific performance constraints. A series of case studies demonstrates the capabilities of the framework. Various component models can be seamlessly incorporated, including new robot types, task profiles, and probabilistic sensing objectives, while non-obvious design alternatives are systematically uncovered with optimality guarantees. The results highlight the flexibility, scalability, and interpretability of the proposed approach, and illustrate how formal co-design enables principled reasoning about complex heterogeneous multi-robot systems.

</details>


### [189] [VistaBot: View-Robust Robot Manipulation via Spatiotemporal-Aware View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21914)
*Songen Gu,Yuhang Zheng,Weize Li,Yupeng Zheng,Yating Feng,Xiang Li,Yilun Chen,Pengfei Li,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VistaBot是一个融合前馈几何模型与视频扩散模型的框架，通过4D几何估计、视角合成潜在提取和潜在动作学习三个核心组件，在不需测试时相机标定的情况下实现视角鲁棒的闭环操控，在ACT和π₀策略上分别提升视角泛化分数2.79倍和2.63倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端机器人操控模型在固定相机训练下对相机视角变化的鲁棒性不足，限制了其在实际部署中的泛化能力。

Method: 提出VistaBot框架，包含三个关键组件：4D几何估计、视角合成潜在提取和潜在动作学习；将该框架集成到动作分块（ACT）和基于扩散的策略（π₀）中；引入视角泛化分数（VGS）作为新的评估指标。

Result: VistaBot在VGS指标上相比ACT和π₀分别提升2.79倍和2.63倍，同时实现高质量的新视角合成。

Conclusion: 本文贡献包括几何感知合成模型、潜在动作规划器、新的基准评估指标以及在多样环境中的广泛验证，代码和模型将开源。

Abstract: Recently, end-to-end robotic manipulation models have gained significant attention for their generalizability and scalability. However, they often suffer from limited robustness to camera viewpoint changes when training with a fixed camera. In this paper, we propose VistaBot, a novel framework that integrates feed-forward geometric models with video diffusion models to achieve view-robust closed-loop manipulation without requiring camera calibration at test time. Our approach consists of three key components: 4D geometry estimation, view synthesis latent extraction, and latent action learning. VistaBot is integrated into both action-chunking (ACT) and diffusion-based ($π_0$) policies and evaluated across simulation and real-world tasks. We further introduce the View Generalization Score (VGS) as a new metric for comprehensive evaluation of cross-view generalization. Results show that VistaBot improves VGS by 2.79$\times$ and 2.63$\times$ over ACT and $π_0$, respectively, while also achieving high-quality novel view synthesis. Our contributions include a geometry-aware synthesis model, a latent action planner, a new benchmark metric, and extensive validation across diverse environments. The code and models will be made publicly available.

</details>
